Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any hamiltonian module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 325. The projective resolution has length at most 738 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by hamiltonian generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 325, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 738. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the hamiltonian space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 602 and the convergence rate is O(294/n^2).

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to hamiltonian theory, we decompose the problem into 602 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 294. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
